PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#@#
###

'''
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```xml
<domain>
    (define (domain sokoban)
        (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
        (:types player box position)
        (:predicates
            (at ?entity - (player box) ?pos - position)
            (is_goal ?pos - position)
            (is_wall ?pos - position)
            (adjacent ?pos1 ?pos2 - position)
        )
        
        (:action move_left
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action move_right
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action move_up
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action move_down
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action push_left
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )

        (:action push_right
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )

        (:action push_up
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )

        (:action push_down
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )
    )
</domain>
```

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban)
        (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
        (:types player box position)
        (:predicates
            (at ?entity - (player box) ?pos - position)
            (is_goal ?pos - position)
            (is_wall ?pos - position)
            (adjacent ?pos1 ?pos2 - position)
        )
        
        (:action move_left
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action move_right
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action move_up
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action move_down
            :parameters (?entity - player ?from ?to - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?entity ?from)
                (adjacent ?from ?to)
                (not (is_wall ?to))
                (not (at box_1 ?to))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?entity ?from))
                (at ?entity ?to)
            )
        )

        (:action push_left
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )

        (:action push_right
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )

        (:action push_up
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)
            :precondition (and
                (at ?player ?p1)
                (at ?box ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p1 ?p2)
                (adjacent ?p2 ?p3)
                (not (is_wall ?p3))
                (not (at box_1 ?p3))
            )
            :effect (and
                (not (at ?player ?p1))
                (at ?player ?p2)
                (not (at ?box ?p2))
                (at ?box ?p3)
            )
        )

        (:action push_down
            :parameters (?player - player ?box - box ?p1 ?p2 ?p3 - position)

PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem sokoban-problem)
        (:domain sokoban)
        (:objects
            player_1 - player
            box_1 - box
            pos_0 pos_1 pos_2 pos_3 pos_4 pos_5 pos_6 pos_7 pos_8 pos_9
            pos_10 pos_11 pos_12 pos_13 pos_14 pos_15 pos_16 pos_17 pos_18 pos_19
            pos_20 pos_21 pos_22 pos_23 pos_24 pos_25 pos_26 pos_27 pos_28 pos_29

            pos_80 pos_81 pos_82 pos_83 pos_84 pos_85 pos_86 pos_87 pos_88 pos_89
            pos_90 pos_91 pos_92 pos_93 pos_94 pos_95 - position
        )
        (:init
            (at player_1 pos_90)
            (at box_1 pos_87)
            (is_goal pos_2)
            (is_wall pos_0) (is_wall pos_1) (is_wall pos_3) (is_wall pos_4) (is_wall pos_5)
            (is_wall pos_6) (is_wall pos_7) (is_wall pos_8) (is_wall pos_9)
            (is_wall pos_10) (is_wall pos_12) (is_wall pos_14) (is_wall pos_16)
            (is_wall pos_18) (is_wall pos_20) (is_wall pos_22) (is_wall pos_24)
            (is_wall pos_26) (is_wall pos_28) (is_wall pos_30) (is_wall pos_32)
            (is_wall pos_34) (is_wall pos_36) (is_wall pos_38) (is_wall pos_40)
            (is_wall pos_42) (is_wall pos_44) (is_wall pos_46) (is_wall pos_48)
            (is_wall pos_50) (is_wall pos_52) (is_wall pos_54) (is_wall pos_56)
            (is_wall pos_58) (is_wall pos_60) (is_wall pos_62) (is_wall pos_64)

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#@#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.

'''

'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''

D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```<domain>
(define (domain sokoban)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types player_type box_type goal_type cell_type)
  (:predicates 
    (player_at ?p - player_type ?c - cell_type)
    (box_at ?b - box_type ?c - cell_type)
    (goal_at ?g - goal_type ?c - cell_type)
    (empty ?c - cell_type)
    (adjacent ?c1 - cell_type ?c2 - cell_type)
  )
  (:action MovePlayer
    :parameters (?p - player_type ?from - cell_type ?to - cell_type)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (empty ?to)
      (adjacent ?from ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?to)
    )
  )
  (:action PushBox
    :parameters (?p - player_type ?b - box_type ?from - cell_type ?box_dest - cell_type ?player_dest - cell_type)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (box_at ?b ?box_dest)
      (adjacent ?from ?box_dest)
      (adjacent ?box_dest ?player_dest)
      (empty ?player_dest)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at ?b ?box_dest))
      (box_at ?b ?player_dest)
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?box_dest)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem sokoban-instance)
  (:domain sokoban)
  (:objects
    player - player_type
    box - box_type
    goal - goal_type
    cell_1_1 cell_45_2 cell_98_2 - cell_type
    ; Add other cell names here for completeness
  )
  (:init
    (player_at player cell_1_1)
    (box_at box cell_45_2)
    (goal_at goal cell_98_2)
    (empty cell_x_y) ; List all empty cells excluding wall positions
    ; Define all adjacency relations for the cells
    (adjacent cell_x_y cell_xprime_yprime) ; Use actual cell coordinates
  )
  (:goal
    (box_at box cell_98_2)
  )
)
</problem>
```
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types player_type box_type goal_type cell_type)
  (:predicates 
    (player_at ?p - player_type ?c - cell_type)
    (box_at ?b - box_type ?c - cell_type)
    (goal_at ?g - goal_type ?c - cell_type)
    (empty ?c - cell_type)
    (adjacent ?c1 - cell_type ?c2 - cell_type)
  )
  (:action MovePlayer
    :parameters (?p - player_type ?from - cell_type ?to - cell_type)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (empty ?to)
      (adjacent ?from ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?to)
    )
  )
  (:action PushBox
    :parameters (?p - player_type ?b - box_type ?from - cell_type ?box_dest - cell_type ?player_dest - cell_type)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (box_at ?b ?box_dest)
      (adjacent ?from ?box_dest)
      (adjacent ?box_dest ?player_dest)
      (empty ?player_dest)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at ?b ?box_dest))
      (box_at ?b ?player_dest)
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?box_dest)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem sokoban-instance)
  (:domain sokoban)
  (:objects
    player - player_type
    box - box_type
    goal - goal_type
    cell_1_1 cell_45_2 cell_98_2 - cell_type
    ; Add other cell names here for completeness
  )
  (:init
    (player_at player cell_1_1)
    (box_at box cell_45_2)
    (goal_at goal cell_98_2)
    (empty cell_x_y) ; List all empty cells excluding wall positions
    ; Define all adjacency relations for the cells
    (adjacent cell_x_y cell_xprime_yprime) ; Use actual cell coordinates
  )
  (:goal
    (box_at box cell_98_2)
  )
)

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

FINAL-PLAN

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types entity position wall)
  (:predicates
    (at_player ?pos - position)
    (at_box ?pos - position)
    (is_goal ?pos - position)
    (wall ?pos - position)
    (adjacent ?pos1 ?pos2 - position)
  )
  (:action move_player
    :parameters (?from ?to - position)
    :precondition (and (at_player ?from) (adjacent ?from ?to) (not (wall ?to)))
    :effect (and (not (at_player ?from)) (at_player ?to))
  )
  (:action push_box
    :parameters (?player_pos ?box_from ?box_to - position)
    :precondition (and (at_player ?player_pos) (at_box ?box_from) (adjacent ?player_pos ?box_from) (adjacent ?box_from ?box_to) (not (wall ?box_to)))
    :effect (and (not (at_box ?box_from)) (at_box ?box_to) (not (at_player ?player_pos)) (at_player ?box_from))
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>

    p51 p52 p53 p54 p55
    p56 p57 p58 p59 p60

    p81 p82 p83 p84 p85
    p86 p87 p88 p89 p90
    p91 p92 p93 p94 p95
    p96 p97 p98 p99 p100
    - position
    wall_p1 wall_p2 wall_p3
    wall_p4 wall_p5 wall_p6
    wall_p7 wall_p8 wall_p9
    - wall
  )
  (:init
    (at_player p93)
    (at_box p97)
    (is_goal p3)
    (wall p1) (wall p2) (wall p3)
    (wall p4) (wall p5) (wall p6)
```

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types entity position wall)
  (:predicates
    (at_player ?pos - position)
    (at_box ?pos - position)
    (is_goal ?pos - position)
    (wall ?pos - position)
    (adjacent ?pos1 ?pos2 - position)
  )
  (:action move_player
    :parameters (?from ?to - position)
    :precondition (and (at_player ?from) (adjacent ?from ?to) (not (wall ?to)))
    :effect (and (not (at_player ?from)) (at_player ?to))
  )
  (:action push_box
    :parameters (?player_pos ?box_from ?box_to - position)
    :precondition (and (at_player ?player_pos) (at_box ?box_from) (adjacent ?player_pos ?box_from) (adjacent ?box_from ?box_to) (not (wall ?box_to)))
    :effect (and (not (at_box ?box_from)) (at_box ?box_to) (not (at_player ?player_pos)) (at_player ?box_from))
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To solve the Sokoban puzzle described in the PDDL specification, follow these steps to move the box from its initial location to the goal position:

1. **Action: Push Box from Position 4 to Position 3**
   - **Starting point**: The player begins at position 5, and the box is positioned at location 4.
   - **Goal**: Move the box located at position 4 to the goal at position 3.
   - **Details**: The player situated at position 5 pushes the box from its current position (4) directly toward the goal position (3), which is adjacent.

This action accomplishes the task of placing the box at its goal destination, adhering precisely to the constraints given in the plan. The plan involves a single step executed with a unit cost, indicating a straightforward push operation within the defined spatial environment.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```<domain>
(define (domain sokoban)
    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
    (:types entity cell wall floor goal - cell)
    (:predicates
        (at ?e - entity ?c - cell)
        (is-goal ?c - cell)
        (adjacent ?c1 ?c2 - cell)
        (clear ?c - cell)
        (is-box ?e - entity)
        (is-player ?e - entity)
        (on-goal ?box - entity)
    )
    (:action move
        :parameters (?from - cell ?to - cell)
        :precondition (and
            (at player ?from)
            (clear ?to)
            (adjacent ?from ?to)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (at player ?from))
            (at player ?to)
        )
    )
    (:action push
        :parameters (?from - cell ?to - cell ?box_loc - cell)
        :precondition (and
            (at player ?from)
            (adjacent ?from ?box_loc)
            (at box1 ?box_loc)
            (adjacent ?box_loc ?to)
            (clear ?to)
            (is-box box1)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (at player ?from))
            (not (at box1 ?box_loc))
            (at player ?box_loc)
            (at box1 ?to)
            (when (is-goal ?box_loc) (not (on-goal box1)))
            (when (is-goal ?to) (on-goal box1))
        )
    )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem sokoban-problem)
    (:domain sokoban)
    (:objects
        player - entity
        box1 - entity
        pos_r1_c1 pos_r2_c1 pos_r49_c1 pos_r50_c1 pos_r50_c2 pos_r50_c3 pos_r51_c2 pos_r51_c3 pos_r52_c2 - cell
        wall_r1_c1 wall_r2_c1 wall_r51_c3 wall_r52_c3 - wall
        goal_r50_c2 - goal
    )
    (:init
        (is-player player)
        (is-box box1)
        (at player pos_r2_c1)
        (at box1 pos_r50_c3)
        (is-goal goal_r50_c2)
        (adjacent pos_r1_c1 pos_r2_c1)
        (adjacent pos_r2_c1 pos_r49_c1)
        (adjacent pos_r49_c1 pos_r50_c1)
        (adjacent pos_r50_c1 pos_r50_c2)
        (adjacent pos_r50_c2 pos_r50_c3)
        (adjacent pos_r51_c2 pos_r50_c3)
        (adjacent pos_r51_c3 pos_r51_c2)
        (adjacent pos_r52_c2 pos_r51_c2)
        (clear pos_r1_c1)
        (clear pos_r49_c1)
        (clear pos_r50_c1)
        (clear pos_r50_c2)
        (clear pos_r51_c2)
        (clear pos_r51_c3)
        (clear pos_r52_c2)
    )
    (:goal
        (on-goal box1)
    )
)
</problem>
```
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban)
    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
    (:types entity cell wall floor goal - cell)
    (:predicates
        (at ?e - entity ?c - cell)
        (is-goal ?c - cell)
        (adjacent ?c1 ?c2 - cell)
        (clear ?c - cell)
        (is-box ?e - entity)
        (is-player ?e - entity)
        (on-goal ?box - entity)
    )
    (:action move
        :parameters (?from - cell ?to - cell)
        :precondition (and
            (at player ?from)
            (clear ?to)
            (adjacent ?from ?to)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (at player ?from))
            (at player ?to)
        )
    )
    (:action push
        :parameters (?from - cell ?to - cell ?box_loc - cell)
        :precondition (and
            (at player ?from)
            (adjacent ?from ?box_loc)
            (at box1 ?box_loc)
            (adjacent ?box_loc ?to)
            (clear ?to)
            (is-box box1)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (at player ?from))
            (not (at box1 ?box_loc))
            (at player ?box_loc)
            (at box1 ?to)
            (when (is-goal ?box_loc) (not (on-goal box1)))
            (when (is-goal ?to) (on-goal box1))
        )
    )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem sokoban-problem)
    (:domain sokoban)
    (:objects
        player - entity
        box1 - entity
        pos_r1_c1 pos_r2_c1 pos_r49_c1 pos_r50_c1 pos_r50_c2 pos_r50_c3 pos_r51_c2 pos_r51_c3 pos_r52_c2 - cell
        wall_r1_c1 wall_r2_c1 wall_r51_c3 wall_r52_c3 - wall
        goal_r50_c2 - goal
    )
    (:init
        (is-player player)
        (is-box box1)
        (at player pos_r2_c1)
        (at box1 pos_r50_c3)
        (is-goal goal_r50_c2)
        (adjacent pos_r1_c1 pos_r2_c1)
        (adjacent pos_r2_c1 pos_r49_c1)
        (adjacent pos_r49_c1 pos_r50_c1)
        (adjacent pos_r50_c1 pos_r50_c2)
        (adjacent pos_r50_c2 pos_r50_c3)
        (adjacent pos_r51_c2 pos_r50_c3)
        (adjacent pos_r51_c3 pos_r51_c2)
        (adjacent pos_r52_c2 pos_r51_c2)
        (clear pos_r1_c1)
        (clear pos_r49_c1)
        (clear pos_r50_c1)
        (clear pos_r50_c2)
        (clear pos_r51_c2)
        (clear pos_r51_c3)
        (clear pos_r52_c2)
    )
    (:goal
        (on-goal box1)
    )
)
